bnodestore.h:
    1|       |// B+ node store
    2|       |//
    3|       |// Stores fixed-capacity B+ tree nodes in blocks allocated from a shared PagePool.
    4|       |// Each node occupies one block; released blocks can be reused for nodes or keys.
    5|       |//
    6|       |// Manages node allocation, initialization, and release.
    7|       |// Tracks nodes by age and exposes the oldest reclamation candidate.
    8|       |// Node age can change after allocation, so allocation order is not age order.
    9|       |//
   10|       |// Tree navigation, splits, separators, and parent-child and leaf links are
   11|       |// maintained by the caller. A node must be detached from the tree and removed
   12|       |// from the age index before its block is returned to the pool.
   13|       |//
   14|       |// The caller determines the commit-version cutoff for reclamation.
   15|       |
   16|       |#pragma once
   17|       |#include "pagepool.h"
   18|       |#include "common.h"
   19|       |#include "keystore.h"
   20|       |#include <bit>
   21|       |#include <cassert>
   22|       |#include <cstddef>
   23|       |#include <cstdint>
   24|       |#include <cstring>
   25|       |#include <limits>
   26|       |#include <stdexcept>
   27|       |#include <string_view>
   28|       |#include <optional>
   29|       |#include <type_traits>
   30|       |
   31|       | 
   32|       |enum class BNodeKind : std::uint8_t {
   33|       |	Leaf,
   34|       |	Internal
   35|       |};
   36|       |
   37|       |// Defined below; declared here so that a node can point to its parent.
   38|       |struct InternalBlock;
   39|       |
   40|       |struct BNodeHeader {
   41|       |	Version maxCV = 0;
   42|       |	// Set when the node is allocated, from a counter that only grows, and
   43|       |	// cleared when it is released, so that a node recorded with its
   44|       |	// generation can be told apart from one released since. It must start
   45|       |	// at byte sizeof(FreeBlock), 8, or later: the pool keeps a released
   46|       |	// block in its free list by writing the link, a FreeBlock, over its
   47|       |	// first bytes, which would overwrite the cleared generation.
   48|       |	std::uint64_t generation;
   49|       |	BNodeHeader* agePrevious = nullptr;
   50|       |	BNodeHeader* ageNext = nullptr;
   51|       |	InternalBlock* parent = nullptr;
   52|       |	BNodeKind kind;
   53|       |	std::uint8_t count = 0;
   54|       |	std::uint8_t parentSlot = 0;
   55|       |	std::uint8_t childrenNeedingSplit = 0;
   56|       |	// Rank of this node in its parent. Requires a parent.
   57|       |	inline std::size_t parentRank() const;
   58|       |};
   59|       |
   60|       |static_assert(offsetof(BNodeHeader, generation) >= sizeof(FreeBlock),
   61|       |			  "a released block's free-list link must not overwrite the generation");
   62|       |
   63|       |// The free slots of a node of capacity C: bit i is set when slot i is free.
   64|       |// Up to 64 slots it is a single word, and every operation is the one on a
   65|       |// plain uint64_t; larger nodes take one word per 64 slots. setFree and
   66|       |// unsetFree touch one word; any, count and first look at every word.
   67|       |template<std::size_t C>
   68|       |struct FreeSlots {
   69|       |	static constexpr std::size_t Words = (C + 63) / 64;
   70|       |
   71|       |	// Word w of a map with every slot free. Every shift is below 64, even in
   72|       |	// the branch not taken.
   73|   272k|	inline static constexpr std::uint64_t fullWord(std::size_t w) {
   74|   272k|		return w + 1 < Words || C % 64 == 0 ? UINT64_MAX : (std::uint64_t{1} << (C % 64)) - 1;

  145|       |// count, parentSlot, childrenNeedingSplit and order hold slots and ranks in
  146|       |// a std::uint8_t, so a node has at most 255 slots.
  147|       |inline constexpr std::size_t MaxNodeCapacity = 255;
  148|       |
  149|       |template<std::size_t C>
  150|       |struct InternalBlockLayout {
  151|       |	static_assert(C > 0 && C <= MaxNodeCapacity);

  160|       |	std::uint8_t order[C]{};
  161|       |

pagepool.h:
    1|       |// Page pool
    2|       |//
    3|       |// Manages aligned pages divided into fixed-size blocks under a fixed memory budget.
    4|       |// Each block can hold either a B+ tree node or a block of key records.
    5|       |// Both uses share the same free-block list, so released blocks can be reused
    6|       |// for either purpose.
    7|       |//
    8|       |// Provides block allocation and release, and counts the free blocks, so
    9|       |// that callers can reclaim history until enough blocks are available.
   10|       |// History reclamation is handled by the caller.
   11|       |#ifndef RESOLVER1_PAGEPOOL_H
   12|       |#define RESOLVER1_PAGEPOOL_H
   13|       |
   14|       |#include <cstddef>
   15|       |#include <cstdint>
   16|       |#include "common.h"
   17|       |
   18|       |struct FreeBlock {
   19|       |	FreeBlock* next;
   20|       |};
   21|       |
   22|       |struct alignas(PageSize) Page {
   23|       |	std::byte data[PageSize];
   24|       |};
   25|       |
   26|       |static_assert(sizeof(Page) == PageSize);
   27|       |
   28|       |class PagePool {
   29|       |public:
   30|       |	enum class Backing { Legacy, BasePages, TransparentHugePages, HugeTLB };
   31|       |	// RESOLVER1_PAGE_MODE: legacy (default), base, thp, or hugetlb (2 MiB).
   32|       |	// Explicit modes fail on allocation/advice errors; there is no fallback.
   33|       |	PagePool(std::size_t budget, std::size_t blockSize);
   34|       |	~PagePool();
   35|       |
   36|       |	PagePool(const PagePool&) = delete;
   37|       |	PagePool& operator=(const PagePool&) = delete;
   38|       |
   39|       |	std::byte* allocateBlock();
   40|       |	void releaseBlock(void* block);
   41|       |
   42|  8.08k|	std::size_t blockSize() const { return blockSize_; }
   43|      5|	std::size_t budget() const { return budget_; }
   44|     18|	std::size_t allocatedBytes() const { return numPages_ * PageSize; }
   45|       |	// Free blocks, whichever store released them.
   46|   278k|	std::size_t availableBlocks() const { return availableBlocks_; }
   47|      8|	Backing backing() const { return backing_; }
   48|      0|	const void* backingAddress() const { return pages_; }
   49|      8|	std::size_t backingBytes() const { return mappedBytes_ == 0 ? allocatedBytes() : mappedBytes_; }

  | 1762|       |	// One dispatch per resident task per round separates a hint from its use.
  | 1763|       |	// Local continuations can run in the same visit; scans yield at leaf boundaries.
  | 1764|   262k|	enum class Step : std::uint8_t {
  | 1765|   262k|		Empty,             // Unused slot.
  | 1766|   262k|		InspectNode,       // Inspect a hinted node and initialize its search bounds.
  | 1767|   262k|		CompareKeys,       // Consume hints for a separator and midpoint read begin.
  | 1768|   262k|		CompareLeafKey,    // Consume hints for a leaf entry and first read begin.
  | 1769|   262k|		FindPrev,          // Inspect a hinted left leaf for a live predecessor.
  | 1770|   262k|		CheckPrev,         // Compare the hinted predecessor end and read begin.
  | 1771|   262k|		StartScan,         // Initialize unresolved reads at the saved scan position.
  | 1772|   262k|		ScanLeaf,          // Scan unresolved reads against the current leaf.
  | 1773|   262k|		Done               // Completed; retired at round end.

  | 1826|   262k|	auto buildBeginIndexes = [&]() {
  | 1827|   262k|		if (points.size() >= RangeEntry::WriteFlag) {

  | 1829|   262k|		}
  | 1830|   262k|		const uint32_t pointsSize = static_cast<uint32_t>(points.size());
  | 1831|   262k|		uint32_t lastRead = pointsSize;
  | 1832|   262k|		uint32_t lastWrite = pointsSize;
  | 1833|   262k|		for (uint32_t p = 0; p < pointsSize; ++p) {
  | 1834|   262k|			const KeyInfo& point = points[p];

  | 1862|   262k|			nextBegin.push_back(pointsSize);

  | 1865|   262k|			if (last != pointsSize) nextBegin[last] = index;

  | 1867|       |			// the opposite type. Consecutive begins of the same type skip the loop.

  | 1885|   262k|	auto markConflict = [&](uint32_t readIndex) {
  | 1886|   262k|		const RangeEntry& entry = readWriteBegins[readIndex];

  | 1892|   262k|		return reads[readWriteBegins[rank].rangeIndex];
  | 1893|   262k|	};

  | 1902|   262k|	auto readStillNeeded = [&](uint32_t rank) -> bool {
  | 1903|   262k|		const RangeEntry& entry = readWriteBegins[rank];
  | 1904|   262k|		if (transactionInfo[entry.transaction]->tooOld ||
  | 1905|   262k|			conflicts[entry.rangeIndex]) return false;
  | 1906|   262k|		return !transactionConflictStatus[entry.transaction] ||
  | 1907|   262k|			reads[entry.rangeIndex].conflictingKeyRange != nullptr;
  | 1908|   262k|	};
  | 1909|       |
  | 1910|       |	// Read ends are not sorted, so a resolved read can be anywhere in the list.
  | 1911|       |	// Unlink it without moving the begin-sorted records or changing routing bounds.
  | 1912|   262k|	auto removeScanRead = [&](Task& task) {
  | 1913|   262k|		assert(task.readIndex < task.opEnd);
  | 1914|   262k|		const uint32_t next =
  | 1915|   262k|			std::min(nextBegin[task.readIndex], task.opEnd);
  | 1916|   262k|		assert(next > task.readIndex);
  | 1917|   262k|		if (task.readPrev == task.opEnd) {
  | 1918|   262k|			task.readHead = next;
  | 1919|   262k|		} else {
  | 1920|   262k|			nextBegin[task.readPrev] = next;
  | 1921|   262k|		}
  | 1922|   262k|		task.readIndex = next;
  | 1923|   262k|	};
  | 1924|       |
  | 1925|       |	// Compact before the first saved rank in this batch. With a fixed cutoff
  | 1926|       |	// and no writes, later visits preserve saved ranks. Empty leaves remain
  | 1927|       |	// linked and allocated until every traversal cursor has been retired.
  | 1928|   262k|	auto cleanReadLeaf = [&](LeafBlock* leaf) {
  | 1929|   262k|		if (leaf->lastCleanReadBatch == readBatch_) return;
  | 1930|   262k|		cleanExpired(leaf);
  | 1931|   262k|		leaf->lastCleanReadBatch = readBatch_;
  | 1932|   262k|		if (leaf->header.count == 0) {
  | 1933|   262k|			pending_.emplace_back(&leaf->header, leaf->header.generation);
  | 1934|   262k|		}
  | 1935|   262k|	};
  | 1936|       |
  | 1937|   262k|	auto initializeRootTask = [&]() {
  | 1938|   262k|		Task& task = tasks[0];
  | 1939|   262k|		task.node.header = root_;
  | 1940|   262k|		task.opBegin = task.opLo = 0;
  | 1941|   262k|		task.opEnd = task.opHi = readWriteBegins.size();
  | 1942|   262k|		task.step = Step::InspectNode;
  | 1943|       |
  | 1944|   262k|		order[0] = 0;
  | 1945|   262k|		freeSlots.unsetFree(0);
  | 1946|   262k|		active = 1;
  | 1947|   262k|	};
  | 1948|       |
  | 1949|   262k|	auto prefetchBlock = [](NodeRef node) {
  | 1950|   262k|		const char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(node.header);
  | 1951|   262k|		for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < BlockSize; offset += 64) {
  | 1952|   262k|			__builtin_prefetch(bytes + offset);
  | 1953|   262k|		}
  | 1954|   262k|	};
  | 1955|       |
  | 1956|       |	// Check generation validity before reading any linked-block header.
  | 1957|       |	// Locate the requested key bytes past headers and issue at most two hints;
  | 1958|       |	// locating a key in later blocks can still require several header reads.
  | 1959|   262k|	auto prefetchStoredKeySlow = [&](const RangeRef& range,
  | 1960|   262k|							 std::size_t start, std::size_t length) {
  | 1961|   262k|		if (length == 0 || !keyStore_.isValid(range)) return;
  | 1962|   262k|		const KeyBlockHeader* block = range.firstBlock;
  | 1963|   262k|		std::size_t offset = range.keyOffset;
  | 1964|   262k|		while (start >= BlockSize - offset) {
  | 1965|   262k|			start -= BlockSize - offset;
  | 1966|   262k|			block = block->nextBlock;
  | 1967|   262k|			offset = sizeof(KeyBlockHeader);
  | 1968|   262k|		}
  | 1969|   262k|		offset += start;
  | 1970|       |
  | 1971|   262k|		for (unsigned line = 0; line < 2; ++line) {
  | 1972|   262k|			const char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(block) + offset;
  | 1973|   262k|			__builtin_prefetch(bytes);
  | 1974|   262k|			const std::size_t lineBytes =
  | 1975|   262k|				64 - (reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(bytes) & 63);
  | 1976|   262k|			const std::size_t consumed =
  | 1977|   262k|				std::min({length, lineBytes, BlockSize - offset});
  | 1978|   262k|			length -= consumed;
  | 1979|   262k|			if (length == 0 || line == 1) return;
  | 1980|   262k|			offset += consumed;
  | 1981|   262k|			if (offset == BlockSize) {
  | 1982|   262k|				block = block->nextBlock;
  | 1983|   262k|				offset = sizeof(KeyBlockHeader);
  | 1984|   262k|			}
  | 1985|   262k|		}
  | 1986|   262k|	};
  | 1987|       |
  | 1988|       |	// Requires live referenced bytes throughout the access. If the hinted
  | 1989|       |	// lines fit in the first block, compute addresses without reading its header.
  | 1990|       |	// At most two lines are hinted; the rest of a long key may still miss.
  | 1991|   262k|	auto prefetchStoredKey = [&](const RangeRef& range,
  | 1992|   262k|							 std::size_t start, std::size_t length) {
  | 1993|   262k|		if (length == 0) return;
  | 1994|   262k|		const std::size_t offset = range.keyOffset + start;
  | 1995|   262k|		if (offset < BlockSize) {
  | 1996|   262k|			const std::size_t lineBytes = 64 - (offset & 63);
  | 1997|   262k|			const char* bytes =
  | 1998|   262k|				reinterpret_cast<const char*>(range.firstBlock) + offset;
  | 1999|   262k|			if (length <= lineBytes) {
  | 2000|   262k|				__builtin_prefetch(bytes);
  | 2001|   262k|				return;
  | 2002|   262k|			}
  | 2003|   262k|			if (offset + lineBytes < BlockSize) {
  | 2004|   262k|				__builtin_prefetch(bytes);
  | 2005|   262k|				__builtin_prefetch(bytes + lineBytes);
  | 2006|   262k|				return;
  | 2007|   262k|			}
  | 2008|   262k|		}
  | 2009|   262k|		prefetchStoredKeySlow(range, start, length);
  | 2010|   262k|	};
  | 2011|       |
  | 2012|   262k|	auto prefetchRangeBegin = [&](const RangeRef& range) {
  | 2013|   262k|		prefetchStoredKey(range, 0, range.beginLength);
  | 2014|   262k|	};
  | 2015|       |
  | 2016|   262k|	auto prefetchRangeEnd = [&](const RangeRef& range) {
  | 2017|   262k|		prefetchStoredKey(range, range.endStart, range.endLength);
  | 2018|   262k|	};
  | 2019|       |
  | 2020|   262k|	auto prefetchSepKey = [&](NodeRef node, std::size_t rank) {
  | 2021|   262k|		const auto slot = node.internal->order[rank];
  | 2022|   262k|		prefetchRangeBegin(node.internal->separators[slot]);
  | 2023|   262k|	};
  | 2024|       |
  | 2025|   262k|	auto prefetchKey = [](const StringRef& key) {
  | 2026|   262k|		if (key.empty()) return;
  | 2027|   262k|		const auto* bytes = key.begin();
  | 2028|   262k|		__builtin_prefetch(bytes);
  | 2029|   262k|		const std::size_t lineBytes =
  | 2030|   262k|			64 - (reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(bytes) & 63);
  | 2031|   262k|		if (static_cast<std::size_t>(key.size()) > lineBytes) {
  | 2032|   262k|			__builtin_prefetch(bytes + lineBytes);
  | 2033|   262k|		}
  | 2034|   262k|	};
  | 2035|       |
  | 2036|   262k|	auto prefetchReadKey = [&](uint32_t rank) {
  | 2037|   262k|		prefetchKey(readAt(rank).begin);
  | 2038|   262k|	};
  | 2039|       |
  | 2040|       |	// Continuations issue hints and set the state that consumes them on a
  | 2041|       |	// later visit. Prefetch completion is not guaranteed.
  | 2042|   262k|	auto toInspectNode = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2043|   262k|		prefetchBlock(task.node);
  | 2044|   262k|		task.step = Step::InspectNode;
  | 2045|   262k|	};
  | 2046|       |
  | 2047|   262k|	auto toCompareKeys = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2048|   262k|		auto* node = task.node.internal;
  | 2049|   262k|		const int32_t mid = std::midpoint(task.lo, task.hi);
  | 2050|   262k|		const auto slot = node->order[mid];
  | 2051|   262k|		if (node->separatorsCV[slot] <= reclaimCV_ &&
  | 2052|   262k|			node->separatorsCV[slot] != EmptyChildCV) {
  | 2053|       |			// Repair the descriptor before hinting: its old block may be reused.
  | 2054|       |			// Deferred structural cleanup preserves saved internal ranks.
  | 2055|   262k|			refreshSeparatorDeferred(node, mid);
  | 2056|   262k|		}
  | 2057|   262k|		if (node->separatorsCV[slot] != EmptyChildCV) {
  | 2058|   262k|			prefetchSepKey(task.node, mid);
  | 2059|   262k|		}
  | 2060|   262k|		prefetchKey(beginAt(std::midpoint(task.opLo, task.opHi)));
  | 2061|   262k|		task.step = Step::CompareKeys;
  | 2062|   262k|	};
  | 2063|       |
  | 2064|   262k|	auto toCompareLeafKey = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2065|   262k|		const auto* leaf = task.node.leaf;
  | 2066|   262k|		const int32_t mid = std::midpoint(task.lo, task.hi);
  | 2067|   262k|		task.rank = static_cast<uint32_t>(mid);
  | 2068|   262k|		prefetchRangeBegin(leaf->ranges[leaf->order[mid]]);
  | 2069|   262k|		prefetchReadKey(task.readHead);
  | 2070|   262k|		task.step = Step::CompareLeafKey;
  | 2071|   262k|	};
  | 2072|       |
  | 2073|   262k|	auto toCheckPrev = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2074|   262k|		if (task.readIndex == task.readHead) {
  | 2075|   262k|			const auto slot = task.prevLeaf->order[task.prevRank];
  | 2076|   262k|			prefetchRangeEnd(task.prevLeaf->ranges[slot]);
  | 2077|   262k|		}
  | 2078|   262k|		prefetchReadKey(task.readIndex);
  | 2079|   262k|		task.step = Step::CheckPrev;
  | 2080|   262k|	};
  | 2081|       |
  | 2082|   262k|	auto toScanLeaf = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2083|   262k|		prefetchBlock(task.node);
  | 2084|   262k|		task.step = Step::ScanLeaf;
  | 2085|   262k|	};
  | 2086|       |
  | 2087|       |	// Suspension may outlast cache residency. Reissue the resumed state's
  | 2088|       |	// hints without assuming the earlier data is still cached.
  | 2089|   262k|	auto prefetchAgain = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2090|   262k|		switch (task.step) {
  | 2091|   262k|			case Step::InspectNode:
  | 2092|   262k|			case Step::ScanLeaf:
  | 2093|   262k|				prefetchBlock(task.node);
  | 2094|   262k|				break;
  | 2095|   262k|			case Step::CompareKeys:
  | 2096|   262k|				toCompareKeys(task);
  | 2097|   262k|				break;
  | 2098|   262k|			case Step::CompareLeafKey:
  | 2099|   262k|				toCompareLeafKey(task);
  | 2100|   262k|				break;
  | 2101|   262k|			case Step::FindPrev:
  | 2102|   262k|				prefetchBlock(NodeRef{.leaf = task.prevLeaf});
  | 2103|   262k|				break;
  | 2104|   262k|			case Step::CheckPrev:
  | 2105|   262k|				toCheckPrev(task);
  | 2106|   262k|				break;
  | 2107|   262k|			default: break;
  | 2108|   262k|		}
  | 2109|   262k|	};
  | 2110|       |
  | 2111|       |	// Separators [lo, hi) delimit child ranks [lo - 1, hi - 1], inclusive.
  | 2112|       |	// With no boundary left, the entire slice of operations belongs to one child.
  | 2113|   262k|	auto assignChildren = [&](Task& task, int32_t lo, int32_t hi,
  | 2114|   262k|						  uint32_t opBegin, uint32_t opEnd) {
  | 2115|   262k|		task.opBegin = task.opLo = opBegin;
  | 2116|   262k|		task.opEnd = task.opHi = opEnd;
  | 2117|   262k|		if (lo == hi) {
  | 2118|   262k|			const auto slot = task.node.internal->order[lo - 1];
  | 2119|   262k|			task.node.header = task.node.internal->children[slot];
  | 2120|   262k|			toInspectNode(task);
  | 2121|   262k|			return;
  | 2122|   262k|		}
  | 2123|   262k|		task.lo = lo;
  | 2124|   262k|		task.hi = hi;
  | 2125|   262k|		toCompareKeys(task);
  | 2126|   262k|	};
  | 2127|       |
  | 2128|       |	// prevRank is exclusive: decrement before inspecting an entry.
  | 2129|       |	// UINT32_MAX starts at the cleaned count of a newly reached left leaf.
  | 2130|   262k|	auto findPrev = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2131|   262k|		auto* leaf = task.prevLeaf;
  | 2132|   262k|		cleanReadLeaf(leaf);
  | 2133|   262k|		if (task.prevRank == UINT32_MAX) {
  | 2134|   262k|			task.prevRank = leaf->header.count;
  | 2135|   262k|		}
  | 2136|   262k|		while (task.prevRank > 0) {
  | 2137|   262k|			--task.prevRank;
  | 2138|   262k|			const auto slot = leaf->order[task.prevRank];
  | 2139|   262k|			if (leaf->cv[slot] > reclaimCV_) {
  | 2140|   262k|				toCheckPrev(task);
  | 2141|   262k|				return;
  | 2142|   262k|			}
  | 2143|   262k|		}
  | 2144|   262k|		task.prevLeaf = leaf->left;
  | 2145|   262k|		if (task.prevLeaf == nullptr) {
  | 2146|   262k|			task.step = Step::StartScan;
  | 2147|   262k|			return;
  | 2148|   262k|		}
  | 2149|   262k|		prefetchBlock(NodeRef{.leaf = task.prevLeaf});
  | 2150|   262k|		task.prevRank = UINT32_MAX;
  | 2151|   262k|		task.step = Step::FindPrev;
  | 2152|   262k|	};
  | 2153|       |
  | 2154|   262k|	constexpr uint32_t BufferedLeafFlag = uint32_t{1} << 31;
  | 2155|   262k|	uint32_t operationCount = 0;
  | 2156|   262k|	uint32_t writeFrontier = 0;
  | 2157|   262k|	auto bufferTaskLeaf = [&](Task& task, LeafBlock* leaf, uint32_t firstWrite) {
  | 2158|   262k|		if (firstWrite == task.opEnd) return;
  | 2159|   262k|		WriteLeaf group{leaf, firstWrite, task.opEnd, task.splitCandidate};
  | 2160|   262k|		if (task.rightCheckRequired || leaf == nullptr || leaf->header.count == 0) {
  | 2161|   262k|			group.requireRightCheck();
  | 2162|   262k|		}
  | 2163|   262k|		const uint64_t ticket = bufferWriteLeaf(group);
  | 2164|   262k|		assert(ticket < BufferedLeafFlag);
  | 2165|       |		// Read positions retain their next-write links. The first write of
  | 2166|       |		// each disjoint slice names its buffered result once it reaches a leaf.
  | 2167|   262k|		nextOther[firstWrite] = BufferedLeafFlag | static_cast<uint32_t>(ticket);
  | 2168|   262k|	};
  | 2169|   262k|	auto inspectNode = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2170|   262k|		if (task.node.header->kind == BNodeKind::Leaf) {
  | 2171|   262k|			auto* leaf = task.node.leaf;
  | 2172|   262k|			cleanReadLeaf(leaf);
  | 2173|   262k|			if (needsSplit(leaf)) task.splitCandidate = &leaf->header;
  | 2174|   262k|			const uint32_t first = task.opBegin;
  | 2175|   262k|			const uint32_t other =
  | 2176|   262k|				std::min(nextOther[first], task.opEnd);
  | 2177|   262k|			const bool writeFirst = readWriteBegins[first].isWrite();
  | 2178|   262k|			const uint32_t firstWrite = writeFirst ? first : other;
  | 2179|   262k|			task.readHead = writeFirst ? other : first;
  | 2180|   262k|			bufferTaskLeaf(task, leaf, firstWrite);
  | 2181|   262k|			if (task.readHead == task.opEnd) {
  | 2182|   262k|				task.step = Step::Done;
  | 2183|   262k|				completedTasks = true;
  | 2184|   262k|				return;
  | 2185|   262k|			}
  | 2186|   262k|			task.hi = leaf->header.count;
  | 2187|   262k|			task.lo = 0;
  | 2188|   262k|			task.readIndex = task.readHead;
  | 2189|   262k|			if (task.hi == 0) {
  | 2190|   262k|				task.rank = 0;
  | 2191|   262k|				task.prevLeaf = leaf;
  | 2192|   262k|				task.prevRank = 0;
  | 2193|   262k|				findPrev(task);
  | 2194|   262k|			} else {
  | 2195|   262k|				toCompareLeafKey(task);
  | 2196|   262k|			}
  | 2197|   262k|			return;
  | 2198|   262k|		}
  | 2199|   262k|		task.hi = task.node.header->count;
  | 2200|   262k|		if (task.hi == 0) {
  | 2201|   262k|			const uint32_t first = task.opBegin;
  | 2202|   262k|			const uint32_t firstWrite = readWriteBegins[first].isWrite()
  | 2203|   262k|				? first : std::min(nextOther[first], task.opEnd);
  | 2204|   262k|			bufferTaskLeaf(task, nullptr, firstWrite);
  | 2205|   262k|			task.step = Step::Empty;
  | 2206|   262k|			completedTasks = true;
  | 2207|   262k|			return;
  | 2208|   262k|		}
  | 2209|   262k|		if (needsSplit(task.node.header)) task.splitCandidate = task.node.header;
  | 2210|   262k|		assignChildren(task, 1, task.hi,
  | 2211|   262k|					   task.opBegin, task.opEnd);
  | 2212|   262k|	};
  | 2213|       |
  | 2214|   262k|	auto compareKeys = [&](Task& task, uint32_t position) {
  | 2215|       |		// Lower-bound the operation begins: equality belongs on the right, including
  | 2216|       |		// all duplicates, so an equal comparison cannot end the search early.
  | 2217|   262k|		const int32_t mid = std::midpoint(task.lo, task.hi);
  | 2218|   262k|		const uint32_t midRead = std::midpoint(task.opLo, task.opHi);
  | 2219|   262k|		const uint32_t slot = task.node.internal->order[mid];
  | 2220|   262k|		const RangeRef& separator = task.node.internal->separators[slot];
  | 2221|   262k|		if (task.node.internal->separatorsCV[slot] == EmptyChildCV) {
  | 2222|       |			// Empty markers need no live key reference. Route left so forward
  | 2223|       |			// scans retain overlaps beyond even a wholly empty subtree.
  | 2224|   262k|			task.opLo = task.opHi;
  | 2225|   262k|			task.rightCheckRequired = true;
  | 2226|   262k|		} else if (keyStore_.compareBegin(beginAt(midRead), separator) < 0) {
  | 2227|   262k|			task.opLo = midRead + 1;
  | 2228|   262k|		} else {
  | 2229|   262k|			task.opHi = midRead;
  | 2230|   262k|		}
  | 2231|   262k|		if (task.opLo < task.opHi) {
  | 2232|   262k|			toCompareKeys(task);
  | 2233|   262k|			return;
  | 2234|   262k|		}
  | 2235|   262k|		const uint32_t split = task.opLo;
  | 2236|   262k|		if (split == task.opEnd) {
  | 2237|   262k|			assignChildren(task, task.lo, mid, task.opBegin, task.opEnd);
  | 2238|   262k|		} else if (split == task.opBegin) {
  | 2239|   262k|			assignChildren(task, mid + 1, task.hi, task.opBegin, task.opEnd);
  | 2240|   262k|		} else {
  | 2241|       |			// Hint the sibling's first access at creation. It cannot execute
  | 2242|       |			// in this round; admission may suspend it for later rounds.
  | 2243|   262k|			Task right = task;
  | 2244|   262k|			assignChildren(right, mid + 1, task.hi, split, task.opEnd);
  | 2245|   262k|			assignChildren(task, task.lo, mid, task.opBegin, split);
  | 2246|   262k|			newTasks.push_back({position, right});
  | 2247|   262k|		}
  | 2248|   262k|	};
  | 2249|       |
  | 2250|   262k|	auto compareLeafKey = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2251|   262k|		const int32_t mid = static_cast<int32_t>(task.rank);
  | 2252|   262k|		const auto* leaf = task.node.leaf;
  | 2253|   262k|		const auto slot = leaf->order[mid];
  | 2254|   262k|		const int cmp = keyStore_.compareBegin(
  | 2255|   262k|			readAt(task.readHead).begin, leaf->ranges[slot]);
  | 2256|   262k|		if (cmp == 0) {
  | 2257|   262k|			task.rank = mid;
  | 2258|   262k|			task.readIndex = task.readHead;
  | 2259|   262k|			task.step = Step::StartScan;
  | 2260|   262k|			return;
  | 2261|   262k|		}
  | 2262|   262k|		if (cmp > 0) {
  | 2263|   262k|			task.lo = mid + 1;
  | 2264|   262k|		} else {
  | 2265|   262k|			task.hi = mid;
  | 2266|   262k|		}
  | 2267|   262k|		if (task.lo < task.hi) {
  | 2268|   262k|			toCompareLeafKey(task);
  | 2269|   262k|			return;
  | 2270|   262k|		}
  | 2271|   262k|		task.rank = task.lo;
  | 2272|   262k|		task.readIndex = task.readHead;
  | 2273|   262k|		task.prevLeaf = task.node.leaf;
  | 2274|   262k|		task.prevRank = task.rank;
  | 2275|   262k|		findPrev(task);
  | 2276|   262k|	};
  | 2277|       |
  | 2278|   262k|	auto checkPrev = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2279|   262k|		if (readStillNeeded(task.readIndex)) {
  | 2280|   262k|			const auto* leaf = task.prevLeaf;
  | 2281|   262k|			const auto slot = leaf->order[task.prevRank];
  | 2282|   262k|			const auto& read = readAt(task.readIndex);
  | 2283|   262k|			const int cmp = keyStore_.compareEnd(
  | 2284|   262k|				read.begin, leaf->ranges[slot]);
  | 2285|   262k|			if (cmp >= 0) {
  | 2286|       |				// Later reads also begin at or after this write's end.
  | 2287|   262k|				task.step = Step::StartScan;
  | 2288|   262k|				return;
  | 2289|   262k|			}
  | 2290|   262k|			if (leaf->cv[slot] > read.version) {
  | 2291|   262k|				markConflict(task.readIndex);
  | 2292|   262k|			}
  | 2293|   262k|		}
  | 2294|   262k|		task.readIndex=std::min(nextBegin[task.readIndex],task.opEnd);
  | 2295|   262k|		while (task.readIndex < task.opEnd &&
  | 2296|   262k|			   !readStillNeeded(task.readIndex)) {
  | 2297|   262k|			task.readIndex=std::min(nextBegin[task.readIndex],task.opEnd);
  | 2298|   262k|		}
  | 2299|   262k|		if (task.readIndex < task.opEnd) {
  | 2300|   262k|			toCheckPrev(task);
  | 2301|   262k|		} else {
  | 2302|   262k|			task.step = Step::StartScan;
  | 2303|   262k|		}
  | 2304|   262k|	};
  | 2305|       |
  | 2306|       |	// Scan a whole leaf per visit, without dispatching individual pairs.
  | 2307|       |	// Yield at a leaf boundary after hinting the next leaf.
  | 2308|   262k|	auto scanLeaf = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2309|   262k|		auto* leaf = task.node.leaf;
  | 2310|   262k|		cleanReadLeaf(leaf);
  | 2311|   262k|		while (task.rank < leaf->header.count) {
  | 2312|   262k|			const auto slot = leaf->order[task.rank];
  | 2313|   262k|			const auto& write = leaf->ranges[slot];
  | 2314|   262k|			while (task.readIndex < task.opEnd) {
  | 2315|   262k|				if (!readStillNeeded(task.readIndex)) {
  | 2316|   262k|					removeScanRead(task);
  | 2317|   262k|					continue;
  | 2318|   262k|				}
  | 2319|   262k|				const auto& read = readAt(task.readIndex);
  | 2320|   262k|				if (task.readIndex >= task.readReachedEnd) {

  | 1905|    280|			conflicts[entry.rangeIndex]) return false;
  | 1906|    280|		return !transactionConflictStatus[entry.transaction] ||
  | 1907|    280|			reads[entry.rangeIndex].conflictingKeyRange != nullptr;
  | 1908|    280|	};
  | 1909|       |
  | 1910|       |	// Read ends are not sorted, so a resolved read can be anywhere in the list.
  | 1911|       |	// Unlink it without moving the begin-sorted records or changing routing bounds.
  | 1912|    280|	auto removeScanRead = [&](Task& task) {
  | 1913|    280|		assert(task.readIndex < task.opEnd);
  | 1914|    280|		const uint32_t next =
  | 1915|    280|			std::min(nextBegin[task.readIndex], task.opEnd);
  | 1916|    280|		assert(next > task.readIndex);
  | 1917|    280|		if (task.readPrev == task.opEnd) {
  | 1918|    280|			task.readHead = next;
  | 1919|    280|		} else {
  | 1920|    280|			nextBegin[task.readPrev] = next;
  | 1921|    280|		}
  | 1922|    280|		task.readIndex = next;
  | 1923|    280|	};
  | 1924|       |
  | 1925|       |	// Compact before the first saved rank in this batch. With a fixed cutoff
  | 1926|       |	// and no writes, later visits preserve saved ranks. Empty leaves remain
  | 1927|       |	// linked and allocated until every traversal cursor has been retired.
  | 1928|    280|	auto cleanReadLeaf = [&](LeafBlock* leaf) {
  | 1929|    280|		if (leaf->lastCleanReadBatch == readBatch_) return;
  | 1930|    280|		cleanExpired(leaf);
  | 1931|    280|		leaf->lastCleanReadBatch = readBatch_;
  | 1932|    280|		if (leaf->header.count == 0) {
  | 1933|    280|			pending_.emplace_back(&leaf->header, leaf->header.generation);
  | 1934|    280|		}
  | 1935|    280|	};
  | 1936|       |
  | 1937|    280|	auto initializeRootTask = [&]() {
  | 1938|    280|		Task& task = tasks[0];
  | 1939|    280|		task.node.header = root_;
  | 1940|    280|		task.opBegin = task.opLo = 0;
  | 1941|    280|		task.opEnd = task.opHi = readWriteBegins.size();
  | 1942|    280|		task.step = Step::InspectNode;
  | 1943|       |
  | 1944|    280|		order[0] = 0;
  | 1945|    280|		freeSlots.unsetFree(0);
  | 1946|    280|		active = 1;
  | 1947|    280|	};
  | 1948|       |
  | 1949|    280|	auto prefetchBlock = [](NodeRef node) {
  | 1950|    280|		const char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(node.header);
  | 1951|    280|		for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < BlockSize; offset += 64) {
  | 1952|    280|			__builtin_prefetch(bytes + offset);
  | 1953|    280|		}
  | 1954|    280|	};
  | 1955|       |
  | 1956|       |	// Check generation validity before reading any linked-block header.
  | 1957|       |	// Locate the requested key bytes past headers and issue at most two hints;
  | 1958|       |	// locating a key in later blocks can still require several header reads.
  | 1959|    280|	auto prefetchStoredKeySlow = [&](const RangeRef& range,
  | 1960|    280|							 std::size_t start, std::size_t length) {
  | 1961|    280|		if (length == 0 || !keyStore_.isValid(range)) return;
  | 1962|    280|		const KeyBlockHeader* block = range.firstBlock;
  | 1963|    280|		std::size_t offset = range.keyOffset;
  | 1964|    280|		while (start >= BlockSize - offset) {
  | 1965|    280|			start -= BlockSize - offset;
  | 1966|    280|			block = block->nextBlock;
  | 1967|    280|			offset = sizeof(KeyBlockHeader);
  | 1968|    280|		}
  | 1969|    280|		offset += start;
  | 1970|       |
  | 1971|    280|		for (unsigned line = 0; line < 2; ++line) {
  | 1972|    280|			const char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(block) + offset;
  | 1973|    280|			__builtin_prefetch(bytes);
  | 1974|    280|			const std::size_t lineBytes =
  | 1975|    280|				64 - (reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(bytes) & 63);
  | 1976|    280|			const std::size_t consumed =
  | 1977|    280|				std::min({length, lineBytes, BlockSize - offset});
  | 1978|    280|			length -= consumed;
  | 1979|    280|			if (length == 0 || line == 1) return;
  | 1980|    280|			offset += consumed;
  | 1981|    280|			if (offset == BlockSize) {
  | 1982|    280|				block = block->nextBlock;
  | 1983|    280|				offset = sizeof(KeyBlockHeader);
  | 1984|    280|			}
  | 1985|    280|		}
  | 1986|    280|	};
  | 1987|       |
  | 1988|       |	// Requires live referenced bytes throughout the access. If the hinted
  | 1989|       |	// lines fit in the first block, compute addresses without reading its header.
  | 1990|       |	// At most two lines are hinted; the rest of a long key may still miss.
  | 1991|    280|	auto prefetchStoredKey = [&](const RangeRef& range,
  | 1992|    280|							 std::size_t start, std::size_t length) {
  | 1993|    280|		if (length == 0) return;
  | 1994|    280|		const std::size_t offset = range.keyOffset + start;
  | 1995|    280|		if (offset < BlockSize) {
  | 1996|    280|			const std::size_t lineBytes = 64 - (offset & 63);
  | 1997|    280|			const char* bytes =
  | 1998|    280|				reinterpret_cast<const char*>(range.firstBlock) + offset;
  | 1999|    280|			if (length <= lineBytes) {
  | 2000|    280|				__builtin_prefetch(bytes);
  | 2001|    280|				return;
  | 2002|    280|			}
  | 2003|    280|			if (offset + lineBytes < BlockSize) {
  | 2004|    280|				__builtin_prefetch(bytes);
  | 2005|    280|				__builtin_prefetch(bytes + lineBytes);
  | 2006|    280|				return;
  | 2007|    280|			}
  | 2008|    280|		}
  | 2009|    280|		prefetchStoredKeySlow(range, start, length);
  | 2010|    280|	};
  | 2011|       |
  | 2012|    280|	auto prefetchRangeBegin = [&](const RangeRef& range) {
  | 2013|    280|		prefetchStoredKey(range, 0, range.beginLength);
  | 2014|    280|	};
  | 2015|       |
  | 2016|    280|	auto prefetchRangeEnd = [&](const RangeRef& range) {
  | 2017|    280|		prefetchStoredKey(range, range.endStart, range.endLength);
  | 2018|    280|	};
  | 2019|       |
  | 2020|    280|	auto prefetchSepKey = [&](NodeRef node, std::size_t rank) {
  | 2021|    280|		const auto slot = node.internal->order[rank];
  | 2022|    280|		prefetchRangeBegin(node.internal->separators[slot]);
  | 2023|    280|	};
  | 2024|       |
  | 2025|    280|	auto prefetchKey = [](const StringRef& key) {
  | 2026|    280|		if (key.empty()) return;
  | 2027|    280|		const auto* bytes = key.begin();
  | 2028|    280|		__builtin_prefetch(bytes);
  | 2029|    280|		const std::size_t lineBytes =
  | 2030|    280|			64 - (reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(bytes) & 63);
  | 2031|    280|		if (static_cast<std::size_t>(key.size()) > lineBytes) {
  | 2032|    280|			__builtin_prefetch(bytes + lineBytes);
  | 2033|    280|		}
  | 2034|    280|	};
  | 2035|       |
  | 2036|    280|	auto prefetchReadKey = [&](uint32_t rank) {
  | 2037|    280|		prefetchKey(readAt(rank).begin);
  | 2038|    280|	};
  | 2039|       |
  | 2040|       |	// Continuations issue hints and set the state that consumes them on a
  | 2041|       |	// later visit. Prefetch completion is not guaranteed.
  | 2042|    280|	auto toInspectNode = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2043|    280|		prefetchBlock(task.node);
  | 2044|    280|		task.step = Step::InspectNode;
  | 2045|    280|	};
  | 2046|       |
  | 2047|    280|	auto toCompareKeys = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2048|    280|		auto* node = task.node.internal;
  | 2049|    280|		const int32_t mid = std::midpoint(task.lo, task.hi);
  | 2050|    280|		const auto slot = node->order[mid];
  | 2051|    280|		if (node->separatorsCV[slot] <= reclaimCV_ &&
  | 2052|    280|			node->separatorsCV[slot] != EmptyChildCV) {
  | 2053|       |			// Repair the descriptor before hinting: its old block may be reused.
  | 2054|       |			// Deferred structural cleanup preserves saved internal ranks.
  | 2055|    280|			refreshSeparatorDeferred(node, mid);
  | 2056|    280|		}
  | 2057|    280|		if (node->separatorsCV[slot] != EmptyChildCV) {
  | 2058|    280|			prefetchSepKey(task.node, mid);
  | 2059|    280|		}
  | 2060|    280|		prefetchKey(beginAt(std::midpoint(task.opLo, task.opHi)));
  | 2061|    280|		task.step = Step::CompareKeys;
  | 2062|    280|	};
  | 2063|       |
  | 2064|    280|	auto toCompareLeafKey = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2065|    280|		const auto* leaf = task.node.leaf;
  | 2066|    280|		const int32_t mid = std::midpoint(task.lo, task.hi);
  | 2067|    280|		task.rank = static_cast<uint32_t>(mid);
  | 2068|    280|		prefetchRangeBegin(leaf->ranges[leaf->order[mid]]);
  | 2069|    280|		prefetchReadKey(task.readHead);
  | 2070|    280|		task.step = Step::CompareLeafKey;
  | 2071|    280|	};
  | 2072|       |
  | 2073|    280|	auto toCheckPrev = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2074|    280|		if (task.readIndex == task.readHead) {
  | 2075|    280|			const auto slot = task.prevLeaf->order[task.prevRank];
  | 2076|    280|			prefetchRangeEnd(task.prevLeaf->ranges[slot]);
  | 2077|    280|		}
  | 2078|    280|		prefetchReadKey(task.readIndex);
  | 2079|    280|		task.step = Step::CheckPrev;
  | 2080|    280|	};
  | 2081|       |
  | 2082|    280|	auto toScanLeaf = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2083|    280|		prefetchBlock(task.node);
  | 2084|    280|		task.step = Step::ScanLeaf;
  | 2085|    280|	};
  | 2086|       |
  | 2087|       |	// Suspension may outlast cache residency. Reissue the resumed state's
  | 2088|       |	// hints without assuming the earlier data is still cached.
  | 2089|    280|	auto prefetchAgain = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2090|    280|		switch (task.step) {
  | 2091|    280|			case Step::InspectNode:
  | 2092|    280|			case Step::ScanLeaf:
  | 2093|    280|				prefetchBlock(task.node);
  | 2094|    280|				break;
  | 2095|    280|			case Step::CompareKeys:
  | 2096|    280|				toCompareKeys(task);
  | 2097|    280|				break;
  | 2098|    280|			case Step::CompareLeafKey:
  | 2099|    280|				toCompareLeafKey(task);
  | 2100|    280|				break;
  | 2101|    280|			case Step::FindPrev:
  | 2102|    280|				prefetchBlock(NodeRef{.leaf = task.prevLeaf});
  | 2103|    280|				break;
  | 2104|    280|			case Step::CheckPrev:
  | 2105|    280|				toCheckPrev(task);
  | 2106|    280|				break;
  | 2107|    280|			default: break;
  | 2108|    280|		}
  | 2109|    280|	};
  | 2110|       |
  | 2111|       |	// Separators [lo, hi) delimit child ranks [lo - 1, hi - 1], inclusive.
  | 2112|       |	// With no boundary left, the entire slice of operations belongs to one child.
  | 2113|    280|	auto assignChildren = [&](Task& task, int32_t lo, int32_t hi,
  | 2114|    280|						  uint32_t opBegin, uint32_t opEnd) {
  | 2115|    280|		task.opBegin = task.opLo = opBegin;
  | 2116|    280|		task.opEnd = task.opHi = opEnd;
  | 2117|    280|		if (lo == hi) {
  | 2118|    280|			const auto slot = task.node.internal->order[lo - 1];
  | 2119|    280|			task.node.header = task.node.internal->children[slot];
  | 2120|    280|			toInspectNode(task);
  | 2121|    280|			return;
  | 2122|    280|		}
  | 2123|    280|		task.lo = lo;
  | 2124|    280|		task.hi = hi;
  | 2125|    280|		toCompareKeys(task);
  | 2126|    280|	};
  | 2127|       |
  | 2128|       |	// prevRank is exclusive: decrement before inspecting an entry.
  | 2129|       |	// UINT32_MAX starts at the cleaned count of a newly reached left leaf.
  | 2130|    280|	auto findPrev = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2131|    280|		auto* leaf = task.prevLeaf;
  | 2132|    280|		cleanReadLeaf(leaf);
  | 2133|    280|		if (task.prevRank == UINT32_MAX) {
  | 2134|    280|			task.prevRank = leaf->header.count;
  | 2135|    280|		}
  | 2136|    280|		while (task.prevRank > 0) {
  | 2137|    280|			--task.prevRank;
  | 2138|    280|			const auto slot = leaf->order[task.prevRank];
  | 2139|    280|			if (leaf->cv[slot] > reclaimCV_) {
  | 2140|    280|				toCheckPrev(task);
  | 2141|    280|				return;
  | 2142|    280|			}
  | 2143|    280|		}
  | 2144|    280|		task.prevLeaf = leaf->left;
  | 2145|    280|		if (task.prevLeaf == nullptr) {
  | 2146|    280|			task.step = Step::StartScan;
  | 2147|    280|			return;
  | 2148|    280|		}
  | 2149|    280|		prefetchBlock(NodeRef{.leaf = task.prevLeaf});
  | 2150|    280|		task.prevRank = UINT32_MAX;
  | 2151|    280|		task.step = Step::FindPrev;
  | 2152|    280|	};
  | 2153|       |
  | 2154|    280|	constexpr uint32_t BufferedLeafFlag = uint32_t{1} << 31;
  | 2155|    280|	uint32_t operationCount = 0;
  | 2156|    280|	uint32_t writeFrontier = 0;
  | 2157|    280|	auto bufferTaskLeaf = [&](Task& task, LeafBlock* leaf, uint32_t firstWrite) {
  | 2158|    280|		if (firstWrite == task.opEnd) return;
  | 2159|    280|		WriteLeaf group{leaf, firstWrite, task.opEnd, task.splitCandidate};
  | 2160|    280|		if (task.rightCheckRequired || leaf == nullptr || leaf->header.count == 0) {
  | 2161|    280|			group.requireRightCheck();
  | 2162|    280|		}
  | 2163|    280|		const uint64_t ticket = bufferWriteLeaf(group);
  | 2164|    280|		assert(ticket < BufferedLeafFlag);
  | 2165|       |		// Read positions retain their next-write links. The first write of
  | 2166|       |		// each disjoint slice names its buffered result once it reaches a leaf.
  | 2167|    280|		nextOther[firstWrite] = BufferedLeafFlag | static_cast<uint32_t>(ticket);
  | 2168|    280|	};
  | 2169|    280|	auto inspectNode = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2170|    280|		if (task.node.header->kind == BNodeKind::Leaf) {
  | 2171|    280|			auto* leaf = task.node.leaf;
  | 2172|    280|			cleanReadLeaf(leaf);
  | 2173|    280|			if (needsSplit(leaf)) task.splitCandidate = &leaf->header;
  | 2174|    280|			const uint32_t first = task.opBegin;
  | 2175|    280|			const uint32_t other =
  | 2176|    280|				std::min(nextOther[first], task.opEnd);
  | 2177|    280|			const bool writeFirst = readWriteBegins[first].isWrite();
  | 2178|    280|			const uint32_t firstWrite = writeFirst ? first : other;
  | 2179|    280|			task.readHead = writeFirst ? other : first;
  | 2180|    280|			bufferTaskLeaf(task, leaf, firstWrite);
  | 2181|    280|			if (task.readHead == task.opEnd) {
  | 2182|    280|				task.step = Step::Done;
  | 2183|    280|				completedTasks = true;
  | 2184|    280|				return;
  | 2185|    280|			}
  | 2186|    280|			task.hi = leaf->header.count;
  | 2187|    280|			task.lo = 0;
  | 2188|    280|			task.readIndex = task.readHead;
  | 2189|    280|			if (task.hi == 0) {
  | 2190|    280|				task.rank = 0;
  | 2191|    280|				task.prevLeaf = leaf;
  | 2192|    280|				task.prevRank = 0;
  | 2193|    280|				findPrev(task);
  | 2194|    280|			} else {
  | 2195|    280|				toCompareLeafKey(task);
  | 2196|    280|			}
  | 2197|    280|			return;
  | 2198|    280|		}
  | 2199|    280|		task.hi = task.node.header->count;
  | 2200|    280|		if (task.hi == 0) {
  | 2201|    280|			const uint32_t first = task.opBegin;
  | 2202|    280|			const uint32_t firstWrite = readWriteBegins[first].isWrite()
  | 2203|    280|				? first : std::min(nextOther[first], task.opEnd);
  | 2204|    280|			bufferTaskLeaf(task, nullptr, firstWrite);
  | 2205|    280|			task.step = Step::Empty;
  | 2206|    280|			completedTasks = true;
  | 2207|    280|			return;
  | 2208|    280|		}
  | 2209|    280|		if (needsSplit(task.node.header)) task.splitCandidate = task.node.header;
  | 2210|    280|		assignChildren(task, 1, task.hi,
  | 2211|    280|					   task.opBegin, task.opEnd);
  | 2212|    280|	};
  | 2213|       |
  | 2214|    280|	auto compareKeys = [&](Task& task, uint32_t position) {
  | 2215|       |		// Lower-bound the operation begins: equality belongs on the right, including
  | 2216|       |		// all duplicates, so an equal comparison cannot end the search early.
  | 2217|    280|		const int32_t mid = std::midpoint(task.lo, task.hi);
  | 2218|    280|		const uint32_t midRead = std::midpoint(task.opLo, task.opHi);
  | 2219|    280|		const uint32_t slot = task.node.internal->order[mid];
  | 2220|    280|		const RangeRef& separator = task.node.internal->separators[slot];
  | 2221|    280|		if (task.node.internal->separatorsCV[slot] == EmptyChildCV) {
  | 2222|       |			// Empty markers need no live key reference. Route left so forward
  | 2223|       |			// scans retain overlaps beyond even a wholly empty subtree.
  | 2224|    280|			task.opLo = task.opHi;
  | 2225|    280|			task.rightCheckRequired = true;
  | 2226|    280|		} else if (keyStore_.compareBegin(beginAt(midRead), separator) < 0) {
  | 2227|    280|			task.opLo = midRead + 1;
  | 2228|    280|		} else {
  | 2229|    280|			task.opHi = midRead;
  | 2230|    280|		}
  | 2231|    280|		if (task.opLo < task.opHi) {
  | 2232|    280|			toCompareKeys(task);
  | 2233|    280|			return;
  | 2234|    280|		}
  | 2235|    280|		const uint32_t split = task.opLo;
  | 2236|    280|		if (split == task.opEnd) {
  | 2237|    280|			assignChildren(task, task.lo, mid, task.opBegin, task.opEnd);
  | 2238|    280|		} else if (split == task.opBegin) {
  | 2239|    280|			assignChildren(task, mid + 1, task.hi, task.opBegin, task.opEnd);
  | 2240|    280|		} else {
  | 2241|       |			// Hint the sibling's first access at creation. It cannot execute
  | 2242|       |			// in this round; admission may suspend it for later rounds.
  | 2243|    280|			Task right = task;
  | 2244|    280|			assignChildren(right, mid + 1, task.hi, split, task.opEnd);
  | 2245|    280|			assignChildren(task, task.lo, mid, task.opBegin, split);
  | 2246|    280|			newTasks.push_back({position, right});
  | 2247|    280|		}
  | 2248|    280|	};
  | 2249|       |
  | 2250|    280|	auto compareLeafKey = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2251|    280|		const int32_t mid = static_cast<int32_t>(task.rank);
  | 2252|    280|		const auto* leaf = task.node.leaf;
  | 2253|    280|		const auto slot = leaf->order[mid];
  | 2254|    280|		const int cmp = keyStore_.compareBegin(
  | 2255|    280|			readAt(task.readHead).begin, leaf->ranges[slot]);
  | 2256|    280|		if (cmp == 0) {
  | 2257|    280|			task.rank = mid;
  | 2258|    280|			task.readIndex = task.readHead;
  | 2259|    280|			task.step = Step::StartScan;
  | 2260|    280|			return;
  | 2261|    280|		}
  | 2262|    280|		if (cmp > 0) {
  | 2263|    280|			task.lo = mid + 1;
  | 2264|    280|		} else {
  | 2265|    280|			task.hi = mid;
  | 2266|    280|		}
  | 2267|    280|		if (task.lo < task.hi) {
  | 2268|    280|			toCompareLeafKey(task);
  | 2269|    280|			return;
  | 2270|    280|		}
  | 2271|    280|		task.rank = task.lo;
  | 2272|    280|		task.readIndex = task.readHead;
  | 2273|    280|		task.prevLeaf = task.node.leaf;
  | 2274|    280|		task.prevRank = task.rank;
  | 2275|    280|		findPrev(task);
  | 2276|    280|	};
  | 2277|       |
  | 2278|    280|	auto checkPrev = [&](Task& task) {
  | 2279|    280|		if (readStillNeeded(task.readIndex)) {
  | 2280|    280|			const auto* leaf = task.prevLeaf;
  | 2281|    280|			const auto slot = leaf->order[task.prevRank];
  | 2282|    280|			const auto& read = readAt(task.readIndex);
  | 2283|    280|			const int cmp = keyStore_.compareEnd(
  | 2284|    280|				read.begin, leaf->ranges[slot]);
  | 2285|    280|			if (cmp >= 0) {
  | 2286|       |				// Later reads also begin at or after this write's end.
  | 2287|    280|				task.step = Step::StartScan;
  | 2288|    280|				return;
  | 2289|    280|			}
  | 2290|    280|			if (leaf->cv[slot] > read.version) {
  | 2291|    280|				markConflict(task.readIndex);
  | 2292|    280|			}
  | 2293|    280|		}
  | 2294|    280|		task.readIndex=std::min(nextBegin[task.readIndex],task.opEnd);
  | 2295|    280|		while (task.readIndex < task.opEnd &&
  | 2296|    280|			   !readStillNeeded(task.readIndex)) {
  | 2297|    280|			task.readIndex=std::min(nextBegin[task.readIndex],task.opEnd);
  | 2298|    280|		}
  | 2299|    280|		if (task.readIndex < task.opEnd) {
  | 2300|    280|			toCheckPrev(task);
  | 2301|    280|		} else {
  | 2302|    280|			task.step = Step::StartScan;
  | 2303|    280|		}
  | 2304|    280|	};
  | 2305|       |
  | 2306|       |	// Scan a whole leaf per visit, without dispatching individual pairs.
  | 2307|       |	// Yield at a leaf boundary after hinting the next leaf.

    1|       |// Temporal Conflict B+ tree
    2|       |//
    3|       |// Stores write-conflict history ordered by key, with commit versions (CVs)
    4|       |// identifying the age of retained conflict information.
    5|       |//
    6|       |// Uses BNodeStore for node allocation and age tracking, and KeyStore for key
    7|       |// storage. Both stores draw blocks from the same PagePool.
    8|       |//
    9|       |// Routes sorted read and write begins together, checks read conflicts, and
   10|       |// applies accepted writes in groups prepared for their destination leaves.
   11|       |// Input key bytes are borrowed during each call; retained keys are copied
   12|       |// into KeyStore.
   13|       |//
   14|       |// Manages the root, searches, updates, splits, separators, parent-child links,
   15|       |// and links between leaves. Entries remain in physical slots; permutations
   16|       |// maintain their logical key order.
   17|       |//
   18|       |// Only the root leaf may be empty when an operation returns. A leaf emptied
   19|       |// during an operation is removed, with any internal node left without
   20|       |// children, before the operation returns.
   21|       |//
   22|       |// Reclamation advances a commit-version cutoff (reclaimCV). Expired leaf
   23|       |// entries are removed during local cleanup, and expired internal separators
   24|       |// are refreshed before use. Entire expired subtrees can be detached and
   25|       |// released without deleting each entry through a separate root traversal.
   26|       |//
   27|       |// Coordinates reclamation of tree nodes and key blocks when space is needed.
   28|       |// Node maximum CVs are conservative upper bounds and need not decrease when
   29|       |// entries are removed.
   30|       |//
   31|       |// No reclamation may occur while key bytes are being accessed.
   32|       |
   33|       |#pragma once
   34|       |
   35|       |#include "pagepool.h"
   36|       |#include "keystore.h"
   37|       |#include "bnodestore.h"
   38|       |#include "fdbcompat.h"
   39|       |
   40|       |#include <algorithm>
   41|       |#include <array>
   42|       |#include <cstddef>
   43|       |#include <cstdint>
   44|       |#include <optional>
   45|       |#include <span>
   46|       |#include <tuple>
   47|       |#include <utility>
   48|       |#include <vector>
   49|       |
   50|       |namespace temp_conflict_btree_detail {
   51|       |// Preorder of binary partitions over [0, count) in breadth order:
   52|       |// 		midpoint, left, right.
   53|       |// Row count contains count valid ranks; the remaining positions are unused.
   54|       |template<std::size_t Capacity>
   55|      0|constexpr auto makePrefetchOrder() {
   56|      0|	std::array<std::array<std::uint16_t, Capacity>, Capacity + 1> table{};
   57|      0|	struct Interval {
   58|      0|		std::size_t lo;
   59|      0|		std::size_t hi;
   60|      0|	};
   61|      0|	for (std::size_t count = 1; count <= Capacity; ++count) {
   62|      0|		std::array<Interval, Capacity> queue{};
   63|      0|		std::size_t head = 0;
   64|      0|		std::size_t tail = 0;
   65|      0|		std::size_t position = 0;
   66|      0|		queue[tail++] = {0, count};
   67|      0|		while (head < tail) {
   68|      0|			const auto [lo, hi] = queue[head++];
   69|      0|			const std::size_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
   70|      0|			table[count][position++] = static_cast<std::uint16_t>(mid);
   71|      0|			if (lo < mid) queue[tail++] = {lo, mid};
   72|      0|			if (mid + 1 < hi) queue[tail++] = {mid + 1, hi};
   73|      0|		}
   74|      0|	}
   75|      0|	return table;
   76|      0|} // template
   77|       |} //namespace

  101|       |	// The batch, its indices and input key bytes must remain valid through this call.
  102|       |	// All intervals use cv; commit versions must be nondecreasing across calls.
